  debug_reports\DEBUG_components_logout-button.md:9:- **Edge Case**: The

  debug_reports\DEBUG_hooks_use-mobile.md:6:
> debug_reports\DEBUG_hooks_use-mobile.md:7:#### 1. Logic Defects, Edge 
Cases, and Unhandled Promise/Async Failures
  debug_reports\DEBUG_hooks_use-mobile.md:8:
> debug_reports\DEBUG_hooks_use-mobile.md:9:- **Edge Case**: The initial 
state of `isMobile` is `undefined`. This can lead to potential issues if the 
component using this hook relies on `isMobile` being a boolean value 
immediately after rendering.
> debug_reports\DEBUG_hooks_use-mobile.md:10:- **Unhandled Promise/Async 
Failures**: There are no promises or async operations in this hook, so there 
are no unhandled promise/async failures to consider.
  debug_reports\DEBUG_hooks_use-mobile.md:11:
> debug_reports\DEBUG_hooks_use-mobile.md:12:#### 2. Race Conditions, State 
Mutation Bugs, or Memory Leaks
  debug_reports\DEBUG_hooks_use-mobile.md:13:
> debug_reports\DEBUG_hooks_use-mobile.md:14:- **Race Condition**: The 
initial state of `isMobile` is set based on the current window width, but 
there is no guarantee that the media query will have been evaluated before 
the initial state is set. This could potentially lead to a race condition 
where the initial state is set before the media query has a chance to update 
it.
  debug_reports\DEBUG_hooks_use-mobile.md:15:- **State Mutation Bug**: The 
state is updated using `setIsMobile`, which is a standard React state update 
function. There is no indication of a state mutation bug.
> debug_reports\DEBUG_hooks_use-mobile.md:16:- **Memory Leak**: The media 
query listener is added in the `useEffect` hook, but there is no check to 
ensure that the listener is removed if the component is unmounted. This could 
lead to a memory leak if the component is unmounted while the media query 
listener is still active.
  debug_reports\DEBUG_hooks_use-mobile.md:17:
  debug_reports\DEBUG_hooks_use-mobile.md:18:#### 3. Security Flaws
  debug_reports\DEBUG_hooks_use-mobile.md:19:
> debug_reports\DEBUG_hooks_use-mobile.md:20:- **Security Flaws**: There are 
no security flaws in this hook. It does not involve any user input or 
external data that could be used to bypass security measures.

Mutation Bugs, or Memory Leaks
  debug_reports\DEBUG_hooks_use-toast.md:16:
> debug_reports\DEBUG_hooks_use-toast.md:17:- **Race Condition: Toast 
Removal**
> debug_reports\DEBUG_hooks_use-toast.md:18:  - The `addToRemoveQueue` 
function uses `setTimeout` to remove a toast after a delay. If the toast is 
dismissed before the timeout, the timeout will still execute, leading to a 
potential race condition.
  debug_reports\DEBUG_hooks_use-toast.md:19:
  debug_reports\DEBUG_hooks_use-toast.md:20:- **State Mutation Bug: Toast 
Dismissal**
  debug_reports\DEBUG_hooks_use-toast.md:21:  - The `DISMISS_TOAST` action 
sets the `open` property of the toast to `false`. However, it does not remove 
the toast from the state immediately. This could lead to a state mutation bug 
where the toast is still present in the state but not visible.
  debug_reports\DEBUG_hooks_use-toast.md:22:
> debug_reports\DEBUG_hooks_use-toast.md:23:- **Memory Leak: Toast Timeout 
Map**
> debug_reports\DEBUG_hooks_use-toast.md:24:  - The `toastTimeouts` map 
stores the timeouts for each toast. If a toast is dismissed before the 
timeout, the timeout is still stored in the map. This could lead to a memory 
leak if the map grows indefinitely.
